```
    90→    "anthropic": "Anthropic",
    91→    "custom": "Custom endpoint",
    92→}
    93→
    94→_PROVIDER_ALIASES = {
    95→    "glm": "zai",
    96→    "z-ai": "zai",
    97→    "z.ai": "zai",
    98→    "zhipu": "zai",
    99→    "kimi": "kimi-coding",
   100→    "moonshot": "kimi-coding",
   101→    "minimax-china": "minimax-cn",
   102→    "minimax_cn": "minimax-cn",
   103→    "claude": "anthropic",
   104→    "claude-code": "anthropic",
   105→}
   106→
   107→
   108→def model_ids() -> list[str]:
   109→    """Return just the OpenRouter model-id strings."""
   110→    return [mid for mid, _ in OPENROUTER_MODELS]
   111→
   112→
   113→def menu_labels() -> list[str]:
   114→    """Return display labels like 'anthropic/claude-opus-4.6 (recommended)'."""
   115→    labels = []
   116→    for mid, desc in OPENROUTER_MODELS:
   117→        labels.append(f"{mid} ({desc})" if desc else mid)
   118→    return labels
   119→
   120→
   121→# All provider IDs and aliases that are valid for the provider:model syntax.
   122→_KNOWN_PROVIDER_NAMES: set[str] = (
   123→    set(_PROVIDER_LABELS.keys())
   124→    | set(_PROVIDER_ALIASES.keys())
   125→    | {"openrouter", "custom"}
   126→)
   127→
   128→
   129→def list_available_providers() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   130→    """Return info about all providers the user could use with ``provider:model``.
   131→
   132→    Each dict has ``id``, ``label``, and ``aliases``.
   133→    Checks which providers have valid credentials configured.
   134→    """
   135→    # Canonical providers in display order
   136→    _PROVIDER_ORDER = [
   137→        "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex",
   138→        "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn", "anthropic",
   139→    ]
   140→    # Build reverse alias map
   141→    aliases_for: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   142→    for alias, canonical in _PROVIDER_ALIASES.items():
   143→        aliases_for.setdefault(canonical, []).append(alias)
   144→
   145→    result = []
   146→    for pid in _PROVIDER_ORDER:
   147→        label = _PROVIDER_LABELS.get(pid, pid)
   148→        alias_list = aliases_for.get(pid, [])
   149→        # Check if this provider has credentials available
   150→        has_creds = False
   151→        try:
   152→            from hermes_cli.runtime_provider import resolve_runtime_provider
   153→            runtime = resolve_runtime_provider(requested=pid)
   154→            has_creds = bool(runtime.get("api_key"))
   155→        except Exception:
   156→            pass
   157→        result.append({
   158→            "id": pid,
   159→            "label": label,
   160→            "aliases": alias_list,
   161→            "authenticated": has_creds,
   162→        })
   163→    return result
   164→
   165→
   166→def parse_model_input(raw: str, current_provider: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
   167→    """Parse ``/model`` input into ``(provider, model)``.
   168→
   169→    Supports ``provider:model`` syntax to switch providers at runtime::
   170→
   171→        openrouter:anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5  →  ("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5")
   172→        nous:hermes-3                           →  ("nous", "hermes-3")
   173→        anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5             →  (current_provider, "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5")
   174→        gpt-5.4                                 →  (current_provider, "gpt-5.4")
   175→
   176→    The colon is only treated as a provider delimiter if the left side is a
   177→    recognized provider name or alias.  This avoids misinterpreting model names
   178→    that happen to contain colons (e.g. ``anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet:beta``).
   179→
   180→    Returns ``(provider, model)`` where *provider* is either the explicit
   181→    provider from the input or *current_provider* if none was specified.
   182→    """
   183→    stripped = raw.strip()
   184→    colon = stripped.find(":")
   185→    if colon > 0:
   186→        provider_part = stripped[:colon].strip().lower()
   187→        model_part = stripped[colon + 1:].strip()
   188→        if provider_part and model_part and provider_part in _KNOWN_PROVIDER_NAMES:
   189→            return (normalize_provider(provider_part), model_part)
   190→    return (current_provider, stripped)
   191→
   192→
   193→def curated_models_for_provider(provider: Optional[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   194→    """Return ``(model_id, description)`` tuples for a provider's model list.
   195→
   196→    Tries to fetch the live model list from the provider's API first,
   197→    falling back to the static ``_PROVIDER_MODELS`` catalog if the API
   198→    is unreachable.
   199→    """
   200→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   201→    if normalized == "openrouter":
   202→        return list(OPENROUTER_MODELS)
   203→
   204→    # Try live API first (Codex, Nous, etc. all support /models)
   205→    live = provider_model_ids(normalized)
   206→    if live:
   207→        return [(m, "") for m in live]
   208→
   209→    # Fallback to static catalog
   210→    models = _PROVIDER_MODELS.get(normalized, [])
   211→    return [(m, "") for m in models]
   212→
   213→
   214→def normalize_provider(provider: Optional[str]) -> str:
   215→    """Normalize provider aliases to Hermes' canonical provider ids.
   216→
   217→    Note: ``"auto"`` passes through unchanged — use
   218→    ``hermes_cli.auth.resolve_provider()`` to resolve it to a concrete
   219→    provider based on credentials and environment.
   220→    """
   221→    normalized = (provider or "openrouter").strip().lower()
   222→    return _PROVIDER_ALIASES.get(normalized, normalized)
   223→
   224→
   225→def provider_model_ids(provider: Optional[str]) -> list[str]:
   226→    """Return the best known model catalog for a provider.
   227→
   228→    Tries live API endpoints for providers that support them (Codex, Nous),
   229→    falling back to static lists.
   230→    """
   231→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   232→    if normalized == "openrouter":
   233→        return model_ids()
   234→    if normalized == "openai-codex":
   235→        from hermes_cli.codex_models import get_codex_model_ids
   236→
   237→        return get_codex_model_ids()
   238→    if normalized == "nous":
   239→        # Try live Nous Portal /models endpoint
   240→        try:
   241→            from hermes_cli.auth import fetch_nous_models, resolve_nous_runtime_credentials
   242→            creds = resolve_nous_runtime_credentials()
   243→            if creds:
   244→                live = fetch_nous_models(creds.get("api_key", ""), creds.get("base_url", ""))
   245→                if live:
   246→                    return live
   247→        except Exception:
   248→            pass
   249→    if normalized == "anthropic":
   250→        live = _fetch_anthropic_models()
   251→        if live:
   252→            return live
   253→    return list(_PROVIDER_MODELS.get(normalized, []))
   254→
   255→
   256→def _fetch_anthropic_models(timeout: float = 5.0) -> Optional[list[str]]:
   257→    """Fetch available models from the Anthropic /v1/models endpoint.
   258→
   259→    Uses resolve_anthropic_token() to find credentials (env vars or
   260→    Claude Code auto-discovery).  Returns sorted model IDs or None.
   261→    """
   262→    try:
   263→        from agent.anthropic_adapter import resolve_anthropic_token, _is_oauth_token
   264→    except ImportError:
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→    [REDACTED]()
   268→    if not token:
   269→        return None
   270→
   271→    headers: dict[str, str] = {"anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"}
   272→    if _is_oauth_token(token):
   273→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"
   274→        from agent.anthropic_adapter import _COMMON_BETAS, _OAUTH_ONLY_BETAS
   275→        headers["anthropic-beta"] = ",".join(_COMMON_BETAS + _OAUTH_ONLY_BETAS)
   276→    else:
   277→        headers["x-api-key"] = token
   278→
   279→    req = urllib.request.Request(
   280→        "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models",
   281→        headers=headers,
   282→    )
   283→    try:
   284→        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   285→            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
   286→            models = [m["id"] for m in data.get("data", []) if m.get("id")]
   287→            # Sort: latest/largest first (opus > sonnet > haiku, higher version first)
   288→            return sorted(models, key=lambda m: (
   289→                "opus" not in m,      # opus first
   290→                "sonnet" not in m,    # then sonnet
   291→                "haiku" not in m,     # then haiku
   292→                m,                    # alphabetical within tier
   293→            ))
   294→    except Exception as e:
   295→        import logging
   296→        logging.getLogger(__name__).debug("Failed to fetch Anthropic models: %s", e)
   297→        return None
   298→
   299→
   300→def fetch_api_models(
   301→    api_key: Optional[str],
   302→    base_url: Optional[str],
   303→    timeout: float = 5.0,
   304→) -> Optional[list[str]]:
   305→    """Fetch the list of available model IDs from the provider's ``/models`` endpoint.
   306→
   307→    Returns a list of model ID strings, or ``None`` if the endpoint could not
   308→    be reached (network error, timeout, auth failure, etc.).
   309→    """
   310→    if not base_url:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    url = base_url.rstrip("/") + "/models"
   314→    headers: dict[str, str] = {}
   315→    if api_key:
   316→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
   317→
   318→    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
   319→    try:
   320→        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
   321→            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
   322→            # Standard OpenAI format: {"data": [{"id": "model-name", ...}, ...]}
   323→            return [m.get("id", "") for m in data.get("data", [])]
   324→    except Exception:
   325→        return None
   326→
   327→
   328→def validate_requested_model(
   329→    model_name: str,
   330→    provider: Optional[str],
   331→    *,
   332→    api_key: Optional[str] = None,
   333→    base_url: Optional[str] = None,
   334→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   335→    """
   336→    Validate a ``/model`` value for the active provider.
   337→
   338→    Performs format checks first, then probes the live API to confirm
   339→    the model actually exists.
   340→
   341→    Returns a dict with:
   342→      - accepted: whether the CLI should switch to the requested model now
   343→      - persist: whether it is safe to save to config
   344→      - recognized: whether it matched a known provider catalog
   345→      - message: optional warning / guidance for the user
   346→    """
   347→    requested = (model_name or "").strip()
   348→    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
   349→    if normalized == "openrouter" and base_url and "openrouter.ai" not in base_url:
   350→        normalized = "custom"
   351→
   352→    if not requested:
   353→        return {
   354→            "accepted": False,
   355→            "persist": False,
   356→            "recognized": False,
   357→            "message": "Model name cannot be empty.",
   358→        }
   359→
   360→    if any(ch.isspace() for ch in requested):
   361→        return {
   362→            "accepted": False,
   363→            "persist": False,
   364→            "recognized": False,
   365→            "message": "Model names cannot contain spaces.",
   366→        }
   367→
   368→    # Custom endpoints can serve any model — skip validation
   369→    if normalized == "custom":
   370→        return {
   371→            "accepted": True,
   372→            "persist": True,
   373→            "recognized": False,
   374→            "message": None,
   375→        }
   376→
   377→    # Probe the live API to check if the model actually exists
   378→    api_models = fetch_api_models(api_key, base_url)
   379→
   380→    if api_models is not None:
   381→        if requested in set(api_models):
   382→            # API confirmed the model exists
   383→            return {
   384→                "accepted": True,
   385→                "persist": True,
   386→                "recognized": True,
   387→                "message": None,
   388→            }
   389→        else:
   390→            # API responded but model is not listed.  Accept anyway —
   391→            # the user may have access to models not shown in the public
   392→            # listing (e.g. Z.AI Pro/Max plans can use glm-5 on coding
   393→            # endpoints even though it's not in /models).  Warn but allow.
   394→            suggestions = get_close_matches(requested, api_models, n=3, cutoff=0.5)
   395→            suggestion_text = ""
   396→            if suggestions:
   397→                suggestion_text = "\n  Similar models: " + ", ".join(f"`{s}`" for s in suggestions)
   398→
   399→            return {
   400→                "accepted": True,
   401→                "persist": True,
   402→                "recognized": False,
   403→                "message": (
   404→                    f"Note: `{requested}` was not found in this provider's model listing. "
   405→                    f"It may still work if your plan supports it."
   406→                    f"{suggestion_text}"
   407→                ),
   408→            }
   409→
   410→    # api_models is None — couldn't reach API.  Accept and persist,
   411→    # but warn so typos don't silently break things.
   412→    provider_label = _PROVIDER_LABELS.get(normalized, normalized)
   413→    return {
   414→        "accepted": True,
   415→        "persist": True,
   416→        "recognized": False,
   417→        "message": (
   418→            f"Could not reach the {provider_label} API to validate `{requested}`. "
   419→            f"If the service isn't down, this model may not be valid."
   420→        ),
   421→    }
   422→
```

```
1→"""Shared auxiliary OpenAI client for cheap/fast side tasks.
     2→
     3→Provides a single resolution chain so every consumer (context compression,
     4→session search, web extraction, vision analysis, browser vision) picks up
     5→the best available backend without duplicating fallback logic.
     6→
     7→Resolution order for text tasks (auto mode):
     8→  1. OpenRouter  (OPENROUTER_API_KEY)
     9→  2. Nous Portal (~/.hermes/auth.json active provider)
    10→  3. Custom endpoint (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY)
    11→  4. Codex OAuth (Responses API via chatgpt.com with gpt-5.3-codex,
    12→     wrapped to look like a chat.completions client)
    13→  5. Direct API-key providers (z.ai/GLM, Kimi/Moonshot, MiniMax, MiniMax-CN)
    14→     — checked via PROVIDER_REGISTRY entries with auth_type='api_key'
    15→  6. None
    16→
    17→Resolution order for vision/multimodal tasks (auto mode):
    18→  1. OpenRouter
    19→  2. Nous Portal
    20→  3. Codex OAuth (gpt-5.3-codex supports vision via Responses API)
    21→  4. Custom endpoint (for local vision models: Qwen-VL, LLaVA, Pixtral, etc.)
    22→  5. None  (API-key providers like z.ai/Kimi/MiniMax are skipped —
    23→     they may not support multimodal)
    24→
    25→Per-task provider overrides (e.g. AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER,
    26→CONTEXT_COMPRESSION_PROVIDER) can force a specific provider for each task:
    27→"openrouter", "nous", "codex", or "main" (= steps 3-5).
    28→Default "auto" follows the chains above.
    29→
    30→Per-task model overrides (e.g. AUXILIARY_VISION_MODEL,
    31→AUXILIARY_WEB_EXTRACT_MODEL) let callers use a different model slug
    32→than the provider's default.
    33→"""
    34→
    35→import json
    36→import logging
    37→import os
    38→from pathlib import Path
    39→from types import SimpleNamespace
    40→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    41→
    42→from openai import OpenAI
    43→
    44→from hermes_constants import OPENROUTER_BASE_URL
    45→
    46→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    47→
    48→# Default auxiliary models for direct API-key providers (cheap/fast for side tasks)
    49→_API_KEY_PROVIDER_AUX_MODELS: Dict[str, str] = {
    50→    "zai": "glm-4.5-flash",
    51→    "kimi-coding": "kimi-k2-turbo-preview",
    52→    "minimax": "MiniMax-M2.5-highspeed",
    53→    "minimax-cn": "MiniMax-M2.5-highspeed",

    55→}

    58→_OR_HEADERS = {

    60→    "X-OpenRouter-Title": "Hermes Agent",

    63→
    64→# Nous Portal extra_body for product attribution.

    68→

    70→auxiliary_is_nous: bool = False
    71→
    72→# Default auxiliary models per provider
    73→_OPENROUTER_MODEL = "google/gemini-3-flash-preview"
    74→_NOUS_MODEL = "gemini-3-flash"
    75→_NOUS_DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "https://inference-api.nousresearch.com/v1"
    76→_AUTH_JSON_PATH = Path.home() / ".hermes" / "auth.json"
    77→
    78→# Codex fallback: uses the Responses API (the only endpoint the Codex
    79→# OAuth token can access) with a fast model for auxiliary tasks.
    80→_CODEX_AUX_MODEL = "gpt-5.3-codex"
    81→_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL = "https://chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex"

    83→
    84→# ── Codex Responses → chat.completions adapter ─────────────────────────────
    85→# All auxiliary consumers call client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs) and
    86→# read response.choices[0].message.content. This adapter translates those
    87→# calls to the Codex Responses API so callers don't need any changes.
    88→
    89→
    90→def _convert_content_for_responses(content: Any) -> Any:
    91→    """Convert chat.completions content to Responses API format.
    92→
    93→    chat.completions uses:
    94→      {"type": "text", "text": "..."}
    95→      {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": "data:image/png;base64,..."}}
    96→
    97→    Responses API uses:
    98→      {"type": "input_text", "text": "..."}
    99→      {"type": "input_image", "image_url": "data:image/png;base64,..."}
   100→
   101→    If content is a plain string, it's returned as-is (the Responses API
   102→    accepts strings directly for text-only messages).
   103→    """
   104→    if isinstance(content, str):
   105→        return content
   106→    if not isinstance(content, list):
   107→        return str(content) if content else ""
   108→
   109→    converted: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   110→    for part in content:
   111→        if not isinstance(part, dict):
   112→            continue
   113→        ptype = part.get("type", "")
   114→        if ptype == "text":
   115→            converted.append({"type": "input_text", "text": part.get("text", "")})
   116→        elif ptype == "image_url":
   117→            # chat.completions nests the URL: {"image_url": {"url": "..."}}
   118→            image_data = part.get("image_url", {})
   119→            url = image_data.get("url", "") if isinstance(image_data, dict) else str(image_data)
   120→            entry: Dict[str, Any] = {"type": "input_image", "image_url": url}
   121→            # Preserve detail if specified
   122→            detail = image_data.get("detail") if isinstance(image_data, dict) else None
   123→            if detail:
   124→                entry["detail"] = detail
   125→            converted.append(entry)
   126→        elif ptype in ("input_text", "input_image"):
   127→            # Already in Responses format — pass through
   128→            converted.append(part)
   129→        else:
   130→            # Unknown content type — try to preserve as text
   131→            text = part.get("text", "")
   132→            if text:
   133→                converted.append({"type": "input_text", "text": text})
   134→
   135→    return converted or ""
   136→
   137→
   138→class _CodexCompletionsAdapter:
   139→    """Drop-in shim that accepts chat.completions.create() kwargs and
   140→    routes them through the Codex Responses streaming API."""
   141→
   142→    def __init__(self, real_client: OpenAI, model: str):
   143→        self._client = real_client
   144→        self._model = model
   145→
   146→    def create(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
   147→        messages = kwargs.get("messages", [])
   148→        model = kwargs.get("model", self._model)
   149→        temperature = kwargs.get("temperature")
   150→
   151→        # Separate system/instructions from conversation messages.
   152→        # Convert chat.completions multimodal content blocks to Responses
   153→        # API format (input_text / input_image instead of text / image_url).
   154→        instructions = "You are a helpful assistant."
   155→        input_msgs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   156→        for msg in messages:
   157→            role = msg.get("role", "user")
   158→            content = msg.get("content") or ""
   159→            if role == "system":
   160→                instructions = content if isinstance(content, str) else str(content)
   161→            else:
   162→                input_msgs.append({
   163→                    "role": role,
   164→                    "content": _convert_content_for_responses(content),
   165→                })
   166→
   167→        resp_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
   168→            "model": model,
   169→            "instructions": instructions,
   170→            "input": input_msgs or [{"role": "user", "content": ""}],
   171→            "store": False,
   172→        }
   173→
   174→        # Note: the Codex endpoint (chatgpt.com/backend-api/codex) does NOT
   175→        # support max_output_tokens or temperature — omit to avoid 400 errors.
   176→
   177→        # Tools support for flush_memories and similar callers
   178→        tools = kwargs.get("tools")
   179→        if tools:
   180→            converted = []
   181→            for t in tools:
   182→                fn = t.get("function", {}) if isinstance(t, dict) else {}
   183→                name = fn.get("name")
   184→                if not name:
   185→                    continue
   186→                converted.append({
   187→                    "type": "function",
   188→                    "name": name,
   189→                    "description": fn.get("description", ""),
   190→                    "parameters": fn.get("parameters", {}),
   191→                })
   192→            if converted:
   193→                resp_kwargs["tools"] = converted
   194→
   195→        # Stream and collect the response
   196→        text_parts: List[str] = []
   197→        tool_calls_raw: List[Any] = []
   198→        usage = None
   199→
   200→        try:
   201→            with self._client.responses.stream(**resp_kwargs) as stream:
   202→                for _event in stream:
   203→                    pass
   204→                final = stream.get_final_response()
   205→
   206→            # Extract text and tool calls from the Responses output
   207→            for item in getattr(final, "output", []):
   208→                item_type = getattr(item, "type", None)
   209→                if item_type == "message":
   210→                    for part in getattr(item, "content", []):
   211→                        ptype = getattr(part, "type", None)
   212→                        if ptype in ("output_text", "text"):
   213→                            text_parts.append(getattr(part, "text", ""))
   214→                elif item_type == "function_call":
   215→                    tool_calls_raw.append(SimpleNamespace(
   216→                        id=getattr(item, "call_id", ""),
   217→                        type="function",
   218→                        function=SimpleNamespace(
   219→                            name=getattr(item, "name", ""),
   220→                            arguments=getattr(item, "arguments", "{}"),
   221→                        ),
   222→                    ))
   223→
   224→            resp_usage = getattr(final, "usage", None)
   225→            if resp_usage:
   226→                usage = SimpleNamespace(
   227→                    prompt_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "input_tokens", 0),
   228→                    completion_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "output_tokens", 0),
   229→                    total_tokens=getattr(resp_usage, "total_tokens", 0),
   230→                )
   231→        except Exception as exc:
   232→            logger.debug("Codex auxiliary Responses API call failed: %s", exc)
   233→            raise
   234→
   235→        content = "".join(text_parts).strip() or None
   236→
   237→        # Build a response that looks like chat.completions
   238→        message = SimpleNamespace(
   239→            role="assistant",
   240→            content=content,
   241→            tool_calls=tool_calls_raw or None,
   242→        )
   243→        choice = SimpleNamespace(
   244→            index=0,
   245→            message=message,
   246→            finish_reason="stop" if not tool_calls_raw else "tool_calls",
   247→        )
   248→        return SimpleNamespace(
   249→            choices=[choice],
   250→            model=model,
   251→            usage=usage,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→
   255→class _CodexChatShim:
   256→    """Wraps the adapter to provide client.chat.completions.create()."""
   257→
   258→    def __init__(self, adapter: _CodexCompletionsAdapter):
   259→        self.completions = adapter
   260→
   261→
   262→class CodexAuxiliaryClient:
   263→    """OpenAI-client-compatible wrapper that routes through Codex Responses API.
   264→
   265→    Consumers can call client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs) as normal.
   266→    Also exposes .api_key and .base_url for introspection by async wrappers.
   267→    """
   268→
   269→    def __init__(self, real_client: OpenAI, model: str):
   270→        self._real_client = real_client
   271→        adapter = _CodexCompletionsAdapter(real_client, model)
   272→        self.chat = _CodexChatShim(adapter)
   273→        self.[REDACTED]
   274→        self.base_url = real_client.base_url
   275→
   276→    def close(self):
   277→        self._real_client.close()
   278→
   279→
   280→class _AsyncCodexCompletionsAdapter:
   281→    """Async version of the Codex Responses adapter.
   282→
   283→    Wraps the sync adapter via asyncio.to_thread() so async consumers
   284→    (web_tools, session_search) can await it as normal.
   285→    """
   286→
   287→    def __init__(self, sync_adapter: _CodexCompletionsAdapter):
   288→        self._sync = sync_adapter
   289→
   290→    async def create(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
   291→        import asyncio
   292→        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync.create, **kwargs)
   293→
   294→
   295→class _AsyncCodexChatShim:
   296→    def __init__(self, adapter: _AsyncCodexCompletionsAdapter):
   297→        self.completions = adapter
   298→
   299→
   300→class AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient:
   301→    """Async-compatible wrapper matching AsyncOpenAI.chat.completions.create()."""
   302→
   303→    def __init__(self, sync_wrapper: "CodexAuxiliaryClient"):
   304→        sync_adapter = sync_wrapper.chat.completions
   305→        async_adapter = _AsyncCodexCompletionsAdapter(sync_adapter)
   306→        self.chat = _AsyncCodexChatShim(async_adapter)
   307→        self.[REDACTED]
   308→        self.base_url = sync_wrapper.base_url
   309→
   310→
   311→def _read_nous_auth() -> Optional[dict]:
   312→    """Read and validate ~/.hermes/auth.json for an active Nous provider.
   313→
   314→    Returns the provider state dict if Nous is active with tokens,
   315→    otherwise None.
   316→    """
   317→    try:
   318→        if not _AUTH_JSON_PATH.is_file():
   319→            return None
   320→        data = json.loads(_AUTH_JSON_PATH.read_text())
   321→        if data.get("active_provider") != "nous":
   322→            return None
   323→        provider = data.get("providers", {}).get("nous", {})
   324→        # Must have at least an access_token or agent_key
   325→        if not provider.get("agent_key") and not provider.get("access_token"):
   326→            return None
   327→        return provider
   328→    except Exception as exc:
   329→        logger.debug("Could not read Nous auth: %s", exc)
   330→        return None
   331→
   332→
   333→def _nous_api_key(provider: dict) -> str:
   334→    """Extract the best API key from a Nous provider state dict."""
   335→    return provider.get("agent_key") or provider.get("access_token", "")
   336→
   337→
   338→def _nous_base_url() -> str:
   339→    """Resolve the Nous inference base URL from env or default."""
   340→    return os.getenv("NOUS_INFERENCE_BASE_URL", _NOUS_DEFAULT_BASE_URL)
   341→
   342→
   343→def _read_codex_access_token() -> Optional[str]:
   344→    """Read a valid Codex OAuth access token from Hermes auth store (~/.hermes/auth.json)."""
   345→    try:
   346→        from hermes_cli.auth import _read_codex_tokens
   347→        data = _read_codex_tokens()
   348→        tokens = data.get("tokens", {})
   349→        access_token = tokens.get("access_token")
   350→        if isinstance(access_token, str) and access_token.strip():
   351→            return access_token.strip()
   352→        return None
   353→    except Exception as exc:
   354→        logger.debug("Could not read Codex auth for auxiliary client: %s", exc)
   355→        return None
   356→
   357→
   358→def _resolve_api_key_provider() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   359→    """Try each API-key provider in PROVIDER_REGISTRY order.
   360→
   361→    Returns (client, model) for the first provider whose env var is set,
   362→    or (None, None) if none are configured.
   363→    """
   364→    try:
   365→        from hermes_cli.auth import PROVIDER_REGISTRY
   366→    except ImportError:
   367→        logger.debug("Could not import PROVIDER_REGISTRY for API-key fallback")
   368→        return None, None
   369→
   370→    for provider_id, pconfig in PROVIDER_REGISTRY.items():
   371→        if pconfig.auth_type != "api_key":
   372→            continue
   373→        # Check if any of the provider's env vars are set
   374→        api_key = ""
   375→        for env_var in pconfig.api_key_env_vars:
   376→            val = os.getenv(env_var, "").strip()
   377→            if val:
   378→                api_key = val
   379→                break
   380→        if not api_key:
   381→            continue
   382→        # Resolve base URL (with optional env-var override)
   383→        # Kimi Code keys (sk-kimi-) need api.kimi.com/coding/v1
   384→        env_url = ""
   385→        if pconfig.base_url_env_var:
   386→            env_url = os.getenv(pconfig.base_url_env_var, "").strip()
   387→        if env_url:
   388→            base_url = env_url.rstrip("/")
   389→        elif provider_id == "kimi-coding" and api_key.startswith("sk-kimi-"):
   390→            base_url = "https://api.kimi.com/coding/v1"
   391→        else:
   392→            base_url = pconfig.inference_base_url
   393→        model = _API_KEY_PROVIDER_AUX_MODELS.get(provider_id, "default")
   394→        logger.debug("Auxiliary text client: %s (%s)", pconfig.name, model)
   395→        extra = {}
   396→        if "api.kimi.com" in base_url.lower():
   397→            extra["default_headers"] = {"User-Agent": "KimiCLI/1.0"}
   398→        return OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url, **extra), model
   399→
   400→    return None, None
   401→
   402→
   403→# ── Provider resolution helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   404→
   405→def _get_auxiliary_provider(task: str = "") -> str:
   406→    """Read the provider override for a specific auxiliary task.
   407→
   408→    Checks AUXILIARY_{TASK}_PROVIDER first (e.g. AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER),
   409→    then CONTEXT_{TASK}_PROVIDER (for the compression section's summary_provider),
   410→    then falls back to "auto".  Returns one of: "auto", "openrouter", "nous", "main".
   411→    """
   412→    if task:
   413→        for prefix in ("AUXILIARY_", "CONTEXT_"):
   414→            val = os.getenv(f"{prefix}{task.upper()}_PROVIDER", "").strip().lower()
   415→            if val and val != "auto":
   416→                return val
   417→    return "auto"
   418→
   419→
   420→def _try_openrouter() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   421→    or_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
   422→    if not or_key:
   423→        return None, None
   424→    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: OpenRouter")
   425→    return OpenAI(api_key=or_key, base_url=OPENROUTER_BASE_URL,
   426→                   default_headers=_OR_HEADERS), _OPENROUTER_MODEL
   427→
   428→
   429→def _try_nous() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   430→    nous = _read_nous_auth()
   431→    if not nous:
   432→        return None, None
   433→    global auxiliary_is_nous
   434→    auxiliary_is_nous = True
   435→    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: Nous Portal")
   436→    return (
   437→        OpenAI(api_key=_nous_api_key(nous), base_url=_nous_base_url()),
   438→        _NOUS_MODEL,
   439→    )
   440→
   441→
   442→def _try_custom_endpoint() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   443→    custom_base = os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL")
   444→    custom_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
   445→    if not custom_base or not custom_key:
   446→        return None, None
   447→    model = os.getenv("OPENAI_MODEL") or "gpt-4o-mini"
   448→    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: custom endpoint (%s)", model)
   449→    return OpenAI(api_key=custom_key, base_url=custom_base), model
   450→
   451→
   452→def _try_codex() -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   453→    [REDACTED]()
   454→    if not codex_token:
   455→        return None, None
   456→    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: Codex OAuth (%s via Responses API)", _CODEX_AUX_MODEL)
   457→    real_client = OpenAI(api_key=codex_token, base_url=_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL)
   458→    return CodexAuxiliaryClient(real_client, _CODEX_AUX_MODEL), _CODEX_AUX_MODEL
   459→
   460→
   461→def _resolve_forced_provider(forced: str) -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   462→    """Resolve a specific forced provider.  Returns (None, None) if creds missing."""
   463→    if forced == "openrouter":
   464→        client, model = _try_openrouter()
   465→        if client is None:
   466→            logger.warning("auxiliary.provider=openrouter but OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set")
   467→        return client, model
   468→
   469→    if forced == "nous":
   470→        client, model = _try_nous()
   471→        if client is None:
   472→            logger.warning("auxiliary.provider=nous but Nous Portal not configured (run: hermes login)")
   473→        return client, model
   474→
   475→    if forced == "codex":
   476→        client, model = _try_codex()
   477→        if client is None:
   478→            logger.warning("auxiliary.provider=codex but no Codex OAuth token found (run: hermes model)")
   479→        return client, model
   480→
   481→    if forced == "main":
   482→        # "main" = skip OpenRouter/Nous, use the main chat model's credentials.
   483→        for try_fn in (_try_custom_endpoint, _try_codex, _resolve_api_key_provider):
   484→            client, model = try_fn()
   485→            if client is not None:
   486→                return client, model
   487→        logger.warning("auxiliary.provider=main but no main endpoint credentials found")
   488→        return None, None
   489→
   490→    # Unknown provider name — fall through to auto
   491→    logger.warning("Unknown auxiliary.provider=%r, falling back to auto", forced)
   492→    return None, None
   493→
   494→
   495→def _resolve_auto() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   496→    """Full auto-detection chain: OpenRouter → Nous → custom → Codex → API-key → None."""
   497→    for try_fn in (_try_openrouter, _try_nous, _try_custom_endpoint,
   498→                   _try_codex, _resolve_api_key_provider):
   499→        client, model = try_fn()
   500→        if client is not None:
   501→            return client, model
   502→    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: none available")
   503→    return None, None
   504→
   505→
   506→# ── Centralized Provider Router ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   507→#
   508→# resolve_provider_client() is the single entry point for creating a properly
   509→# configured client given a (provider, model) pair.  It handles auth lookup,
   510→# base URL resolution, provider-specific headers, and API format differences
   511→# (Chat Completions vs Responses API for Codex).
   512→#
   513→# All auxiliary consumer code should go through this or the public helpers
   514→# below — never look up auth env vars ad-hoc.
   515→
   516→
   517→def _to_async_client(sync_client, model: str):
   518→    """Convert a sync client to its async counterpart, preserving Codex routing."""
   519→    from openai import AsyncOpenAI
   520→
   521→    if isinstance(sync_client, CodexAuxiliaryClient):
   522→        return AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient(sync_client), model
   523→
   524→    async_kwargs = {
   525→        "[REDACTED],
   526→        "base_url": str(sync_client.base_url),
   527→    }
   528→    base_lower = str(sync_client.base_url).lower()
   529→    if "openrouter" in base_lower:
   530→        async_kwargs["default_headers"] = dict(_OR_HEADERS)
   531→    elif "api.kimi.com" in base_lower:
   532→        async_kwargs["default_headers"] = {"User-Agent": "KimiCLI/1.0"}
   533→    return AsyncOpenAI(**async_kwargs), model
   534→
   535→
   536→def resolve_provider_client(
   537→    provider: str,
   538→    model: str = None,
   539→    async_mode: bool = False,
   540→    raw_codex: bool = False,
   541→) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   542→    """Central router: given a provider name and optional model, return a
   543→    configured client with the correct auth, base URL, and API format.
   544→
   545→    The returned client always exposes ``.chat.completions.create()`` — for
   546→    Codex/Responses API providers, an adapter handles the translation
   547→    transparently.
   548→
   549→    Args:
   550→        provider: Provider identifier.  One of:
   551→            "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex" (or "codex"),
   552→            "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn",
   553→            "custom" (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY),
   554→            "auto" (full auto-detection chain).
   555→        model: Model slug override.  If None, uses the provider's default
   556→               auxiliary model.
   557→        async_mode: If True, return an async-compatible client.
   558→        raw_codex: If True, return a raw OpenAI client for Codex providers
   559→            instead of wrapping in CodexAuxiliaryClient.  Use this when
   560→            the caller needs direct access to responses.stream() (e.g.,
   561→            the main agent loop).
   562→
   563→    Returns:
   564→        (client, resolved_model) or (None, None) if auth is unavailable.
   565→    """
   566→    # Normalise aliases
   567→    provider = (provider or "auto").strip().lower()
   568→    if provider == "codex":
   569→        provider = "openai-codex"
   570→    if provider == "main":
   571→        provider = "custom"
   572→
   573→    # ── Auto: try all providers in priority order ────────────────────
   574→    if provider == "auto":
   575→        client, resolved = _resolve_auto()
   576→        if client is None:
   577→            return None, None
   578→        final_model = model or resolved
   579→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   580→                else (client, final_model))
   581→
   582→    # ── OpenRouter ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   583→    if provider == "openrouter":
   584→        client, default = _try_openrouter()
   585→        if client is None:
   586→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openrouter requested "
   587→                           "but OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set")
   588→            return None, None
   589→        final_model = model or default
   590→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   591→                else (client, final_model))
   592→
   593→    # ── Nous Portal (OAuth) ──────────────────────────────────────────
   594→    if provider == "nous":
   595→        client, default = _try_nous()
   596→        if client is None:
   597→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: nous requested "
   598→                           "but Nous Portal not configured (run: hermes login)")
   599→            return None, None
   600→        final_model = model or default
   601→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   602→                else (client, final_model))
   603→
   604→    # ── OpenAI Codex (OAuth → Responses API) ─────────────────────────
   605→    if provider == "openai-codex":
   606→        if raw_codex:
   607→            # Return the raw OpenAI client for callers that need direct
   608→            # access to responses.stream() (e.g., the main agent loop).
   609→            [REDACTED]()
   610→            if not codex_token:
   611→                logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openai-codex requested "
   612→                               "but no Codex OAuth token found (run: hermes model)")
   613→                return None, None
   614→            final_model = model or _CODEX_AUX_MODEL
   615→            raw_client = OpenAI(api_key=codex_token, base_url=_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL)
   616→            return (raw_client, final_model)
   617→        # Standard path: wrap in CodexAuxiliaryClient adapter
   618→        client, default = _try_codex()
   619→        if client is None:
   620→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openai-codex requested "
   621→                           "but no Codex OAuth token found (run: hermes model)")
   622→            return None, None
   623→        final_model = model or default
   624→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   625→                else (client, final_model))
   626→
   627→    # ── Custom endpoint (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY) ───────────
   628→    if provider == "custom":
   629→        # Try custom first, then codex, then API-key providers
   630→        for try_fn in (_try_custom_endpoint, _try_codex,
   631→                       _resolve_api_key_provider):
   632→            client, default = try_fn()
   633→            if client is not None:
   634→                final_model = model or default
   635→                return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   636→                        else (client, final_model))
   637→        logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: custom/main requested "
   638→                       "but no endpoint credentials found")
   639→        return None, None
   640→
   641→    # ── API-key providers from PROVIDER_REGISTRY ─────────────────────
   642→    try:
   643→        from hermes_cli.auth import PROVIDER_REGISTRY, _resolve_kimi_base_url
   644→    except ImportError:
   645→        logger.debug("hermes_cli.auth not available for provider %s", provider)
   646→        return None, None
   647→
   648→    pconfig = PROVIDER_REGISTRY.get(provider)
   649→    if pconfig is None:
   650→        logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: unknown provider %r", provider)
   651→        return None, None
   652→
   653→    if pconfig.auth_type == "api_key":
   654→        # Find the first configured API key
   655→        api_key = ""
   656→        for env_var in pconfig.api_key_env_vars:
   657→            api_key = os.getenv(env_var, "").strip()
   658→            if api_key:
   659→                break
   660→        if not api_key:
   661→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: provider %s has no API "
   662→                           "key configured (tried: %s)",
   663→                           provider, ", ".join(pconfig.api_key_env_vars))
   664→            return None, None
   665→
   666→        # Resolve base URL (env override → provider-specific logic → default)
   667→        base_url_override = os.getenv(pconfig.base_url_env_var, "").strip() if pconfig.base_url_env_var else ""
   668→        if provider == "kimi-coding":
   669→            base_url = _resolve_kimi_base_url(api_key, pconfig.inference_base_url, base_url_override)
   670→        elif base_url_override:
   671→            base_url = base_url_override
   672→        else:
   673→            base_url = pconfig.inference_base_url
   674→
   675→        default_model = _API_KEY_PROVIDER_AUX_MODELS.get(provider, "")
   676→        final_model = model or default_model
   677→
   678→        # Provider-specific headers
   679→        headers = {}
   680→        if "api.kimi.com" in base_url.lower():
   681→            headers["User-Agent"] = "KimiCLI/1.0"
   682→
   683→        client = OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url,
   684→                        **({"default_headers": headers} if headers else {}))
   685→        logger.debug("resolve_provider_client: %s (%s)", provider, final_model)
   686→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   687→                else (client, final_model))
   688→
   689→    elif pconfig.auth_type in ("oauth_device_code", "oauth_external"):
   690→        # OAuth providers — route through their specific try functions
   691→        if provider == "nous":
   692→            return resolve_provider_client("nous", model, async_mode)
   693→        if provider == "openai-codex":
   694→            return resolve_provider_client("openai-codex", model, async_mode)
   695→        # Other OAuth providers not directly supported
   696→        logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: OAuth provider %s not "
   697→                       "directly supported, try 'auto'", provider)
   698→        return None, None
   699→
   700→    logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: unhandled auth_type %s for %s",
   701→                   pconfig.auth_type, provider)
   702→    return None, None
   703→
   704→
   705→# ── Public API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   706→
   707→def get_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = "") -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   708→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for text-only auxiliary tasks.
   709→
   710→    Args:
   711→        task: Optional task name ("compression", "web_extract") to check
   712→              for a task-specific provider override.
   713→
   714→    Callers may override the returned model with a per-task env var
   715→    (e.g. CONTEXT_COMPRESSION_MODEL, AUXILIARY_WEB_EXTRACT_MODEL).
   716→    """
   717→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   718→    if forced != "auto":
   719→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   720→    return resolve_provider_client("auto")
   721→
   722→
   723→def get_async_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = ""):
   724→    """Return (async_client, model_slug) for async consumers.
   725→
   726→    For standard providers returns (AsyncOpenAI, model). For Codex returns
   727→    (AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient, model) which wraps the Responses API.
   728→    Returns (None, None) when no provider is available.
   729→    """
   730→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   731→    if forced != "auto":
   732→        return resolve_provider_client(forced, async_mode=True)
   733→    return resolve_provider_client("auto", async_mode=True)
   734→
   735→
   736→def get_vision_auxiliary_client() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   737→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for vision/multimodal auxiliary tasks.
   738→
   739→    Checks AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER for a forced provider, otherwise
   740→    auto-detects.  Callers may override the returned model with
   741→    AUXILIARY_VISION_MODEL.
   742→
   743→    In auto mode, only providers known to support multimodal are tried:
   744→    OpenRouter, Nous Portal, and Codex OAuth (gpt-5.3-codex supports
   745→    vision via the Responses API).  Custom endpoints and API-key
   746→    providers are skipped — they may not handle vision input.  To use
   747→    them, set AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER explicitly.
   748→    """
   749→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider("vision")
   750→    if forced != "auto":
   751→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   752→    # Auto: try providers known to support multimodal first, then fall
   753→    # back to the user's custom endpoint.  Many local models (Qwen-VL,
   754→    # LLaVA, Pixtral, etc.) support vision — skipping them entirely
   755→    # caused silent failures for local-only users.
   756→    for try_fn in (_try_openrouter, _try_nous, _try_codex,
   757→                   _try_custom_endpoint):
   758→        client, model = try_fn()
   759→        if client is not None:
   760→            return client, model
   761→    logger.debug("Auxiliary vision client: none available")
   762→    return None, None
   763→
   764→
   765→def get_async_vision_auxiliary_client():
   766→    """Return (async_client, model_slug) for async vision consumers.
   767→
   768→    Properly handles Codex routing — unlike manually constructing
   769→    AsyncOpenAI from a sync client, this preserves the Responses API
   770→    adapter for Codex providers.
   771→
   772→    Returns (None, None) when no provider is available.
   773→    """
   774→    sync_client, model = get_vision_auxiliary_client()
   775→    if sync_client is None:
   776→        return None, None
   777→    return _to_async_client(sync_client, model)
   778→
   779→
   780→def get_auxiliary_extra_body() -> dict:
   781→    """Return extra_body kwargs for auxiliary API calls.
   782→    
   783→    Includes Nous Portal product tags when the auxiliary client is backed
   784→    by Nous Portal. Returns empty dict otherwise.
   785→    """
   786→    return dict(NOUS_EXTRA_BODY) if auxiliary_is_nous else {}
   787→
   788→
   789→def auxiliary_max_tokens_param(value: int) -> dict:
   790→    """Return the correct max tokens kwarg for the auxiliary client's provider.
   791→    
   792→    OpenRouter and local models use 'max_tokens'. Direct OpenAI with newer
   793→    models (gpt-4o, o-series, gpt-5+) requires 'max_completion_tokens'.
   794→    The Codex adapter translates max_tokens internally, so we use max_tokens
   795→    for it as well.
   796→    """
   797→    custom_base = os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL", "")
   798→    or_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
   799→    # Only use max_completion_tokens for direct OpenAI custom endpoints
   800→    if (not or_key
   801→            and _read_nous_auth() is None
   802→            and "api.openai.com" in custom_base.lower()):
   803→        return {"max_completion_tokens": value}
   804→    return {"max_tokens": value}
   805→
   806→
   807→# ── Centralized LLM Call API ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   808→#
   809→# call_llm() and async_call_llm() own the full request lifecycle:
   810→#   1. Resolve provider + model from task config (or explicit args)
   811→#   2. Get or create a cached client for that provider
   812→#   3. Format request args for the provider + model (max_tokens handling, etc.)
   813→#   4. Make the API call
   814→#   5. Return the response
   815→#
   816→# Every auxiliary LLM consumer should use these instead of manually
   817→# constructing clients and calling .chat.completions.create().
   818→
   819→# Client cache: (provider, async_mode) -> (client, default_model)
   820→_client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}
   821→
   822→
   823→def _get_cached_client(
   824→    provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False,
   825→) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   826→    """Get or create a cached client for the given provider."""
   827→    cache_key = (provider, async_mode)
   828→    if cache_key in _client_cache:
   829→        cached_client, cached_default = _client_cache[cache_key]
   830→        return cached_client, model or cached_default
   831→    client, default_model = resolve_provider_client(provider, model, async_mode)
   832→    if client is not None:
   833→        _client_cache[cache_key] = (client, default_model)
   834→    return client, model or default_model
   835→
   836→
   837→def _resolve_task_provider_model(
   838→    task: str = None,
   839→    provider: str = None,
   840→    model: str = None,
   841→) -> Tuple[str, Optional[str]]:
   842→    """Determine provider + model for a call.
   843→
   844→    Priority:
   845→      1. Explicit provider/model args (always win)
   846→      2. Env var overrides (AUXILIARY_{TASK}_PROVIDER, etc.)
   847→      3. Config file (auxiliary.{task}.provider/model or compression.*)
   848→      4. "auto" (full auto-detection chain)
   849→
   850→    Returns (provider, model) where model may be None (use provider default).
   851→    """
   852→    if provider:
   853→        return provider, model
   854→
   855→    if task:
   856→        # Check env var overrides first
   857→        env_provider = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   858→        if env_provider != "auto":
   859→            # Check for env var model override too
   860→            env_model = None
   861→            for prefix in ("AUXILIARY_", "CONTEXT_"):
   862→                val = os.getenv(f"{prefix}{task.upper()}_MODEL", "").strip()
   863→                if val:
   864→                    env_model = val
   865→                    break
   866→            return env_provider, model or env_model
   867→
   868→        # Read from config file
   869→        try:
   870→            from hermes_cli.config import load_config
   871→            config = load_config()
   872→        except ImportError:
   873→            return "auto", model
   874→
   875→        # Check auxiliary.{task} section
   876→        aux = config.get("auxiliary", {})
   877→        task_config = aux.get(task, {})
   878→        cfg_provider = task_config.get("provider", "").strip() or None
   879→        cfg_model = task_config.get("model", "").strip() or None
   880→
   881→        # Backwards compat: compression section has its own keys
   882→        if task == "compression" and not cfg_provider:
   883→            comp = config.get("compression", {})
   884→            cfg_provider = comp.get("summary_provider", "").strip() or None
   885→            cfg_model = cfg_model or comp.get("summary_model", "").strip() or None
   886→
   887→        if cfg_provider and cfg_provider != "auto":
   888→            return cfg_provider, model or cfg_model
   889→        return "auto", model or cfg_model
   890→
   891→    return "auto", model
   892→
   893→
   894→def _build_call_kwargs(
   895→    provider: str,
   896→    model: str,
   897→    messages: list,
   898→    temperature: Optional[float] = None,
   899→    max_tokens: Optional[int] = None,
   900→    tools: Optional[list] = None,
   901→    timeout: float = 30.0,
   902→    extra_body: Optional[dict] = None,
   903→) -> dict:
   904→    """Build kwargs for .chat.completions.create() with model/provider adjustments."""
   905→    kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
   906→        "model": model,
   907→        "messages": messages,
   908→        "timeout": timeout,
   909→    }
   910→
   911→    if temperature is not None:
   912→        kwargs["temperature"] = temperature
   913→
   914→    if max_tokens is not None:
   915→        # Codex adapter handles max_tokens internally; OpenRouter/Nous use max_tokens.
   916→        # Direct OpenAI api.openai.com with newer models needs max_completion_tokens.
   917→        if provider == "custom":
   918→            custom_base = os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL", "")
   919→            if "api.openai.com" in custom_base.lower():
   920→                kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = max_tokens
   921→            else:
   922→                kwargs["max_tokens"] = max_tokens
   923→        else:
   924→            kwargs["max_tokens"] = max_tokens
   925→
   926→    if tools:
   927→        kwargs["tools"] = tools
   928→
   929→    # Provider-specific extra_body
   930→    merged_extra = dict(extra_body or {})
   931→    if provider == "nous" or auxiliary_is_nous:
   932→        merged_extra.setdefault("tags", []).extend(["product=hermes-agent"])
   933→    if merged_extra:
   934→        kwargs["extra_body"] = merged_extra
   935→
   936→    return kwargs
   937→
   938→
   939→def call_llm(
   940→    task: str = None,
   941→    *,
   942→    provider: str = None,
   943→    model: str = None,
   944→    messages: list,
   945→    temperature: float = None,
   946→    max_tokens: int = None,
   947→    tools: list = None,
   948→    timeout: float = 30.0,
   949→    extra_body: dict = None,
   950→) -> Any:
   951→    """Centralized synchronous LLM call.
   952→
   953→    Resolves provider + model (from task config, explicit args, or auto-detect),
   954→    handles auth, request formatting, and model-specific arg adjustments.
   955→
   956→    Args:
   957→        task: Auxiliary task name ("compression", "vision", "web_extract",
   958→              "session_search", "skills_hub", "mcp", "flush_memories").
   959→              Reads provider:model from config/env. Ignored if provider is set.
   960→        provider: Explicit provider override.
   961→        model: Explicit model override.
   962→        messages: Chat messages list.
   963→        temperature: Sampling temperature (None = provider default).
   964→        max_tokens: Max output tokens (handles max_tokens vs max_completion_tokens).
   965→        tools: Tool definitions (for function calling).
   966→        timeout: Request timeout in seconds.
   967→        extra_body: Additional request body fields.
   968→
   969→    Returns:
   970→        Response object with .choices[0].message.content
   971→
   972→    Raises:
   973→        RuntimeError: If no provider is configured.
   974→    """
   975→    resolved_provider, resolved_model = _resolve_task_provider_model(
   976→        task, provider, model)
   977→
   978→    client, final_model = _get_cached_client(resolved_provider, resolved_model)
   979→    if client is None:
   980→        # Fallback: try openrouter
   981→        if resolved_provider != "openrouter":
   982→            logger.warning("Provider %s unavailable, falling back to openrouter",
   983→                           resolved_provider)
   984→            client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
   985→                "openrouter", resolved_model or _OPENROUTER_MODEL)
   986→    if client is None:
   987→        raise RuntimeError(
   988→            f"No LLM provider configured for task={task} provider={resolved_provider}. "
   989→            f"Run: hermes setup")
   990→
   991→    kwargs = _build_call_kwargs(
   992→        resolved_provider, final_model, messages,
   993→        temperature=temperature, max_tokens=max_tokens,
   994→        tools=tools, timeout=timeout, extra_body=extra_body)
   995→
   996→    # Handle max_tokens vs max_completion_tokens retry
   997→    try:
   998→        return client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
   999→    except Exception as first_err:
  1000→        err_str = str(first_err)
  1001→        if "max_tokens" in err_str or "unsupported_parameter" in err_str:
  1002→            kwargs.pop("max_tokens", None)
  1003→            kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = max_tokens
  1004→            return client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
  1005→        raise
  1006→
  1007→
  1008→async def async_call_llm(
  1009→    task: str = None,
  1010→    *,
  1011→    provider: str = None,
  1012→    model: str = None,
  1013→    messages: list,
  1014→    temperature: float = None,
  1015→    max_tokens: int = None,
  1016→    tools: list = None,
  1017→    timeout: float = 30.0,
  1018→    extra_body: dict = None,
  1019→) -> Any:
  1020→    """Centralized asynchronous LLM call.
  1021→
  1022→    Same as call_llm() but async. See call_llm() for full documentation.
  1023→    """
  1024→    resolved_provider, resolved_model = _resolve_task_provider_model(
  1025→        task, provider, model)
  1026→
  1027→    client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
  1028→        resolved_provider, resolved_model, async_mode=True)
  1029→    if client is None:
  1030→        if resolved_provider != "openrouter":
  1031→            logger.warning("Provider %s unavailable, falling back to openrouter",
  1032→                           resolved_provider)
  1033→            client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
  1034→                "openrouter", resolved_model or _OPENROUTER_MODEL,
  1035→                async_mode=True)
  1036→    if client is None:
  1037→        raise RuntimeError(
  1038→            f"No LLM provider configured for task={task} provider={resolved_provider}. "
  1039→            f"Run: hermes setup")
  1040→
  1041→    kwargs = _build_call_kwargs(
  1042→        resolved_provider, final_model, messages,
  1043→        temperature=temperature, max_tokens=max_tokens,
  1044→        tools=tools, timeout=timeout, extra_body=extra_body)
  1045→
  1046→    try:
  1047→        return await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
  1048→    except Exception as first_err:
  1049→        err_str = str(first_err)
  1050→        if "max_tokens" in err_str or "unsupported_parameter" in err_str:
  1051→            kwargs.pop("max_tokens", None)
  1052→            kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = max_tokens
  1053→            return await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
  1054→        raise
  1055→
```

```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Todo Tool Module - Planning & Task Management
     4→
     5→Provides an in-memory task list the agent uses to decompose complex tasks,
     6→track progress, and maintain focus across long conversations. The state
     7→lives on the AIAgent instance (one per session) and is re-injected into
     8→the conversation after context compression events.
     9→
    10→Design:
    11→- Single `todo` tool: provide `todos` param to write, omit to read
    12→- Every call returns the full current list
    13→- No system prompt mutation, no tool response modification
    14→- Behavioral guidance lives entirely in the tool schema description
    15→"""
    16→
    17→import json
    18→from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
    19→
    20→
    21→# Valid status values for todo items
    22→VALID_STATUSES = {"pending", "in_progress", "completed", "cancelled"}
    23→
    24→
    25→class TodoStore:
    26→    """
    27→    In-memory todo list. One instance per AIAgent (one per session).
    28→
    29→    Items are ordered -- list position is priority. Each item has:
    30→      - id: unique string identifier (agent-chosen)
    31→      - content: task description
    32→      - status: pending | in_progress | completed | cancelled
    33→    """
    34→
    35→    def __init__(self):
    36→        self._items: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    37→
    38→    def write(self, todos: List[Dict[str, Any]], merge: bool = False) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    39→        """
    40→        Write todos. Returns the full current list after writing.
    41→
    42→        Args:
    43→            todos: list of {id, content, status} dicts
    44→            merge: if False, replace the entire list. If True, update
    45→                   existing items by id and append new ones.
    46→        """
    47→        if not merge:
    48→            # Replace mode: new list entirely
    49→            self._items = [self._validate(t) for t in todos]
    50→        else:
    51→            # Merge mode: update existing items by id, append new ones
    52→            existing = {item["id"]: item for item in self._items}
    53→            for t in todos:
    54→                item_id = str(t.get("id", "")).strip()
    55→                if not item_id:
    56→                    continue  # Can't merge without an id
    57→
    58→                if item_id in existing:
    59→                    # Update only the fields the LLM actually provided
    60→                    if "content" in t and t["content"]:
    61→                        existing[item_id]["content"] = str(t["content"]).strip()
    62→                    if "status" in t and t["status"]:
    63→                        status = str(t["status"]).strip().lower()
    64→                        if status in VALID_STATUSES:
    65→                            existing[item_id]["status"] = status
    66→                else:
    67→                    # New item -- validate fully and append to end
    68→                    validated = self._validate(t)
    69→                    existing[validated["id"]] = validated
    70→                    self._items.append(validated)
    71→            # Rebuild _items preserving order for existing items
    72→            seen = set()
    73→            rebuilt = []
    74→            for item in self._items:
    75→                current = existing.get(item["id"], item)
    76→                if current["id"] not in seen:
    77→                    rebuilt.append(current)
    78→                    seen.add(current["id"])
    79→            self._items = rebuilt
    80→        return self.read()
    81→
    82→    def read(self) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    83→        """Return a copy of the current list."""
    84→        return [item.copy() for item in self._items]
    85→
    86→    def has_items(self) -> bool:
    87→        """Check if there are any items in the list."""
    88→        return len(self._items) > 0
    89→
    90→    def format_for_injection(self) -> Optional[str]:
    91→        """
    92→        Render the todo list for post-compression injection.
    93→
    94→        Returns a human-readable string to append to the compressed
    95→        message history, or None if the list is empty.
    96→        """
    97→        if not self._items:
    98→            return None
    99→
   100→        # Status markers for compact display
   101→        markers = {
   102→            "completed": "[x]",
   103→            "in_progress": "[>]",
   104→            "pending": "[ ]",
   105→            "cancelled": "[~]",
   106→        }
   107→
   108→        # Only inject pending/in_progress items — completed/cancelled ones
   109→        # cause the model to re-do finished work after compression.
   110→        active_items = [
   111→            item for item in self._items
   112→            if item["status"] in ("pending", "in_progress")
   113→        ]
   114→        if not active_items:
   115→            return None
   116→
   117→        lines = ["[Your active task list was preserved across context compression]"]
   118→        for item in active_items:
   119→            marker = markers.get(item["status"], "[?]")
   120→            lines.append(f"- {marker} {item['id']}. {item['content']} ({item['status']})")
   121→
   122→        return "\n".join(lines)
   123→
   124→    @staticmethod
   125→    def _validate(item: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
   126→        """
   127→        Validate and normalize a todo item.
   128→
   129→        Ensures required fields exist and status is valid.
   130→        Returns a clean dict with only {id, content, status}.
   131→        """
   132→        item_id = str(item.get("id", "")).strip()
   133→        if not item_id:
   134→            item_id = "?"
   135→
   136→        content = str(item.get("content", "")).strip()
   137→        if not content:
   138→            content = "(no description)"
   139→
   140→        status = str(item.get("status", "pending")).strip().lower()
   141→        if status not in VALID_STATUSES:
   142→            status = "pending"
   143→
   144→        return {"id": item_id, "content": content, "status": status}
   145→
   146→
   147→def todo_tool(
   148→    todos: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   149→    merge: bool = False,
   150→    store: Optional[TodoStore] = None,
```

```
150→    store: Optional[TodoStore] = None,
   151→) -> str:
   152→    """
   153→    Single entry point for the todo tool. Reads or writes depending on params.
   154→
   155→    Args:
   156→        todos: if provided, write these items. If None, read current list.
   157→        merge: if True, update by id. If False (default), replace entire list.
   158→        store: the TodoStore instance from the AIAgent.
   159→
   160→    Returns:
   161→        JSON string with the full current list and summary metadata.
   162→    """
   163→    if store is None:
   164→        return json.dumps({"error": "TodoStore not initialized"}, ensure_ascii=False)
   165→
   166→    if todos is not None:
   167→        items = store.write(todos, merge)
   168→    else:
   169→        items = store.read()
   170→
   171→    # Build summary counts
   172→    pending = sum(1 for i in items if i["status"] == "pending")
   173→    in_progress = sum(1 for i in items if i["status"] == "in_progress")
   174→    completed = sum(1 for i in items if i["status"] == "completed")
   175→    cancelled = sum(1 for i in items if i["status"] == "cancelled")
   176→
   177→    return json.dumps({
   178→        "todos": items,
   179→        "summary": {
   180→            "total": len(items),
   181→            "pending": pending,
   182→            "in_progress": in_progress,
   183→            "completed": completed,
```

```
1→"""Central registry for all hermes-agent tools.
     2→
     3→Each tool file calls ``registry.register()`` at module level to declare its
     4→schema, handler, toolset membership, and availability check.  ``model_tools.py``
     5→queries the registry instead of maintaining its own parallel data structures.
     6→
     7→Import chain (circular-import safe):
     8→    tools/registry.py  (no imports from model_tools or tool files)
     9→           ^
    10→    tools/*.py  (import from tools.registry at module level)
    11→           ^
    12→    model_tools.py  (imports tools.registry + all tool modules)
    13→           ^
    14→    run_agent.py, cli.py, batch_runner.py, etc.
    15→"""
    16→
    17→import json
    18→import logging
    19→from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Set
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→class ToolEntry:
    25→    """Metadata for a single registered tool."""
    26→
    27→    __slots__ = (
    28→        "name", "toolset", "schema", "handler", "check_fn",
    29→        "requires_env", "is_async", "description",
    30→    )
    31→
    32→    def __init__(self, name, toolset, schema, handler, check_fn,
    33→                 requires_env, is_async, description):
    34→        self.name = name
    35→        self.toolset = toolset
    36→        self.schema = schema
    37→        self.handler = handler
    38→        self.check_fn = check_fn
    39→        self.requires_env = requires_env
    40→        self.is_async = is_async
    41→        self.description = description
    42→
    43→
    44→class ToolRegistry:
    45→    """Singleton registry that collects tool schemas + handlers from tool files."""
    46→
    47→    def __init__(self):
    48→        self._tools: Dict[str, ToolEntry] = {}
    49→        self._toolset_checks: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
    50→
    51→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→    # Registration
    53→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→    def register(
    56→        self,
    57→        name: str,
    58→        toolset: str,
    59→        schema: dict,
    60→        handler: Callable,
    61→        check_fn: Callable = None,
    62→        requires_env: list = None,
    63→        is_async: bool = False,
    64→        description: str = "",
    65→    ):
    66→        """Register a tool.  Called at module-import time by each tool file."""
    67→        self._tools[name] = ToolEntry(
    68→            name=name,
    69→            toolset=toolset,
    70→            schema=schema,
    71→            handler=handler,
    72→            check_fn=check_fn,
    73→            requires_env=requires_env or [],
    74→            is_async=is_async,
    75→            description=description or schema.get("description", ""),
    76→        )
    77→        if check_fn and toolset not in self._toolset_checks:
    78→            self._toolset_checks[toolset] = check_fn
    79→
    80→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→    # Schema retrieval
    82→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    83→
    84→    def get_definitions(self, tool_names: Set[str], quiet: bool = False) -> List[dict]:
    85→        """Return OpenAI-format tool schemas for the requested tool names.
    86→
    87→        Only tools whose ``check_fn()`` returns True (or have no check_fn)
    88→        are included.
    89→        """
    90→        result = []
    91→        for name in sorted(tool_names):
    92→            entry = self._tools.get(name)
    93→            if not entry:
    94→                continue
    95→            if entry.check_fn:

   100→                        continue
   101→                except Exception:
```

```python
    return registry.dispatch(function_name, function_args, task_id=task_id, user_task=user_task)
```

**Fallback Model Activation (lines 2575-2660):**
This shows the pattern for switching models at runtime:
```python
def _try_activate_fallback(self) -> bool:
    """Switch to fallback model/provider in-place."""
    if self._fallback_activated or not self._fallback_model:
        return False

    fb = self._fallback_model
    fb_provider = (fb.get("provider") or "").strip().lower()
    fb_model = (fb.get("model") or "").strip()
    
    try:
        from agent.auxiliary_client import resolve_provider_client
        fb_client, _ = resolve_provider_client(fb_provider, model=fb_model, raw_codex=True)
        
        # Determine api_mode from provider
        fb_api_mode = "chat_completions"
        if fb_provider == "openai-codex":
            fb_api_mode = "codex_responses"
        elif fb_provider == "anthropic":
            fb_api_mode = "anthropic_messages"
        
        # Swap client and settings in-place
        self.model = fb_model
        self.provider = fb_provider
        self.base_url = fb_base_url
        self.api_mode = fb_api_mode
        self._fallback_activated = True
        
        # Handle Anthropic vs OpenAI clients
        if fb_api_mode == "anthropic_messages":
            self._anthropic_api_key = [REDACTED] = build_anthropic_client(effective_key)
            self.client = None
        else:
            self.client = fb_client
        
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error("Failed to activate fallback model: %s", e)
        return False
```

**List Available Providers (lines 129-163):**
```python
def list_available_providers() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """Return info about all providers the user could use with ``provider:model``.
    
    Each dict has ``id``, ``label``, and ``aliases``.
    Checks which providers have valid credentials configured.
    """
    _PROVIDER_ORDER = [
        "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex",
        "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn", "anthropic",
    ]
    aliases_for: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for alias, canonical in _PROVIDER_ALIASES.items():
        aliases_for.setdefault(canonical, []).append(alias)

    result = []
    for pid in _PROVIDER_ORDER:
        label = _PROVIDER_LABELS.get(pid, pid)
        alias_list = aliases_for.get(pid, [])
        # Check if this provider has credentials available
        has_creds = False
        try:
            from hermes_cli.runtime_provider import resolve_runtime_provider
            runtime = resolve_runtime_provider(requested=pid)
            has_creds = bool(runtime.get("api_key"))
        except Exception:
            pass
        result.append({
            "id": pid,
            "label": label,
            "aliases": alias_list,
            "authenticated": has_creds,
        })
    return result
```

**Get Curated Models for Provider (lines 193-211):**
```python
def curated_models_for_provider(provider: Optional[str]) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Return ``(model_id, description)`` tuples for a provider's model list.
    
    Tries to fetch the live model list from the provider's API first,
    falling back to the static ``_PROVIDER_MODELS`` catalog.
    """
    normalized = normalize_provider(provider)
    if normalized == "openrouter":
        return list(OPENROUTER_MODELS)
    
    # Try live API first
    live = provider_model_ids(normalized)
    if live:
        return [(m, "") for m in live]
    
    # Fallback to static catalog
    models = _PROVIDER_MODELS.get(normalized, [])
    return [(m, "") for m in models]
```

**Provider Lists:**
- **Line 17-31:** OPENROUTER_MODELS - full model catalog
- **Line 33-80:** _PROVIDER_MODELS - per-provider model lists  
- **Line 82-92:** _PROVIDER_LABELS - friendly provider names
- **Line 94-105:** _PROVIDER_ALIASES - alternate provider names

**Central Provider Router (lines 536-702):**

```python
def resolve_provider_client(
    provider: str,
    model: str = None,
    async_mode: bool = False,
    raw_codex: bool = False,
) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
    """Central router: given a provider name and optional model, return a
    configured client with the correct auth, base URL, and API format.
    
    Args:
        provider: Provider identifier. One of:
            "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex" (or "codex"),
            "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn",
            "custom" (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY),
            "auto" (full auto-detection chain).
        model: Model slug override. If None, uses the provider's default.
        async_mode: If True, return an async-compatible client.
        raw_codex: If True, return a raw OpenAI client for Codex providers
            instead of wrapping in CodexAuxiliaryClient.
    
    Returns:
        (client, resolved_model) or (None, None) if auth is unavailable.
    """
    provider = (provider or "auto").strip().lower()
    
    # Auto: try all providers in priority order
    if provider == "auto":
        client, resolved = _resolve_auto()
        if client is None:
            return None, None
        final_model = model or resolved
        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode else (client, final_model))
    
    # OpenRouter
    if provider == "openrouter":
        client, default = _try_openrouter()
        if client is None:
            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openrouter requested but OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set")
            return None, None
        # ...
    
    # ... handles: nous, openai-codex, custom, API-key providers
```

```python
def register(
```
