705→def resolve_provider_credentials(
   706→    provider: str,
   707→    model: str,
   708→) -> Optional[dict]:
   709→    """Resolve a provider+model into a complete credentials bundle.
   710→
   711→    Thin wrapper around :func:`resolve_provider_client` that also derives
   712→    ``api_mode`` from the provider name.  Used by every code path that
   713→    needs to construct or switch to a new provider: fallback activation,
   714→    the ``switch_model`` tool, and per-task delegation overrides.
   715→
   716→    Returns a dict on success::
   717→
   718→        {
   719→            "provider": "openrouter",
   720→            "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6",
   721→            "base_url": "https://...",
   722→            "api_key": "sk-...",
   723→            "api_mode": "chat_completions",
   724→            "client": <OpenAI>,       # ready-to-use client object
   725→        }
   726→
   727→    Returns ``None`` if the provider is not configured (no API key).
   728→    """
   729→    client, _ = resolve_provider_client(provider, model=model, raw_codex=True)
   730→    if client is None:
   731→        return None
   732→
   733→    api_mode = "chat_completions"
   734→    if provider == "openai-codex":
   735→        api_mode = "codex_responses"
   736→    elif provider == "anthropic":
   737→        api_mode = "anthropic_messages"
   738→
   739→    return {
   740→        "provider": provider,
   741→        "model": model,
   742→        "base_url": str(client.base_url),
   743→        "api_key": client.api_key,
   744→        "api_mode": api_mode,
   745→        "client": client,
   746→    }
   747→
   748→
   749→# ── Public API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   750→
   751→def get_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = "") -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   752→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for text-only auxiliary tasks.
   753→
   754→    Args:
   755→        task: Optional task name ("compression", "web_extract") to check
   756→              for a task-specific provider override.
   757→
   758→    Callers may override the returned model with a per-task env var
   759→    (e.g. CONTEXT_COMPRESSION_MODEL, AUXILIARY_WEB_EXTRACT_MODEL).
   760→    """
   761→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   762→    if forced != "auto":
   763→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   764→    return resolve_provider_client("auto")
   765→
   766→
   767→def get_async_text_auxiliary_client(task: str = ""):
   768→    """Return (async_client, model_slug) for async consumers.
   769→
   770→    For standard providers returns (AsyncOpenAI, model). For Codex returns
   771→    (AsyncCodexAuxiliaryClient, model) which wraps the Responses API.
   772→    Returns (None, None) when no provider is available.
   773→    """
   774→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   775→    if forced != "auto":
   776→        return resolve_provider_client(forced, async_mode=True)
   777→    return resolve_provider_client("auto", async_mode=True)
   778→
   779→
   780→def get_vision_auxiliary_client() -> Tuple[Optional[OpenAI], Optional[str]]:
   781→    """Return (client, default_model_slug) for vision/multimodal auxiliary tasks.
   782→
   783→    Checks AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER for a forced provider, otherwise
   784→    auto-detects.  Callers may override the returned model with
   785→    AUXILIARY_VISION_MODEL.
   786→
   787→    In auto mode, only providers known to support multimodal are tried:
   788→    OpenRouter, Nous Portal, and Codex OAuth (gpt-5.3-codex supports
   789→    vision via the Responses API).  Custom endpoints and API-key
   790→    providers are skipped — they may not handle vision input.  To use
   791→    them, set AUXILIARY_VISION_PROVIDER explicitly.
   792→    """
   793→    forced = _get_auxiliary_provider("vision")
   794→    if forced != "auto":
   795→        return resolve_provider_client(forced)
   796→    # Auto: try providers known to support multimodal first, then fall
   797→    # back to the user's custom endpoint.  Many local models (Qwen-VL,
   798→    # LLaVA, Pixtral, etc.) support vision — skipping them entirely
   799→    # caused silent failures for local-only users.
   800→    for try_fn in (_try_openrouter, _try_nous, _try_codex,
   801→                   _try_custom_endpoint):
   802→        client, model = try_fn()
   803→        if client is not None:
   804→            return client, model
   805→    logger.debug("Auxiliary vision client: none available")
   806→    return None, None
   807→
   808→
   809→def get_async_vision_auxiliary_client():
   810→    """Return (async_client, model_slug) for async vision consumers.
   811→
   812→    Properly handles Codex routing — unlike manually constructing
   813→    AsyncOpenAI from a sync client, this preserves the Responses API
   814→    adapter for Codex providers.
   815→
   816→    Returns (None, None) when no provider is available.
   817→    """
   818→    sync_client, model = get_vision_auxiliary_client()
   819→    if sync_client is None:
   820→        return None, None
   821→    return _to_async_client(sync_client, model)
   822→
   823→
   824→def get_auxiliary_extra_body() -> dict:
   825→    """Return extra_body kwargs for auxiliary API calls.
   826→    
   827→    Includes Nous Portal product tags when the auxiliary client is backed
   828→    by Nous Portal. Returns empty dict otherwise.
   829→    """
   830→    return dict(NOUS_EXTRA_BODY) if auxiliary_is_nous else {}
   831→
   832→
   833→def auxiliary_max_tokens_param(value: int) -> dict:
   834→    """Return the correct max tokens kwarg for the auxiliary client's provider.
   835→    
   836→    OpenRouter and local models use 'max_tokens'. Direct OpenAI with newer
   837→    models (gpt-4o, o-series, gpt-5+) requires 'max_completion_tokens'.
   838→    The Codex adapter translates max_tokens internally, so we use max_tokens
   839→    for it as well.
   840→    """
   841→    custom_base = os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL", "")
   842→    or_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
   843→    # Only use max_completion_tokens for direct OpenAI custom endpoints
   844→    if (not or_key
   845→            and _read_nous_auth() is None
   846→            and "api.openai.com" in custom_base.lower()):
   847→        return {"max_completion_tokens": value}
   848→    return {"max_tokens": value}
   849→
   850→
   851→# ── Centralized LLM Call API ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   852→#
   853→# call_llm() and async_call_llm() own the full request lifecycle:
   854→#   1. Resolve provider + model from task config (or explicit args)
   855→#   2. Get or create a cached client for that provider
   856→#   3. Format request args for the provider + model (max_tokens handling, etc.)
   857→#   4. Make the API call
   858→#   5. Return the response
   859→#
   860→# Every auxiliary LLM consumer should use these instead of manually
   861→# constructing clients and calling .chat.completions.create().
   862→
   863→# Client cache: (provider, async_mode) -> (client, default_model)
   864→_client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}
   865→
   866→
   867→def _get_cached_client(
   868→    provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False,
   869→) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   870→    """Get or create a cached client for the given provider."""
   871→    cache_key = (provider, async_mode)
   872→    if cache_key in _client_cache:
   873→        cached_client, cached_default = _client_cache[cache_key]
   874→        return cached_client, model or cached_default
   875→    client, default_model = resolve_provider_client(provider, model, async_mode)
   876→    if client is not None:
   877→        _client_cache[cache_key] = (client, default_model)
   878→    return client, model or default_model
   879→
   880→
   881→def _resolve_task_provider_model(
   882→    task: str = None,
   883→    provider: str = None,
   884→    model: str = None,
   885→) -> Tuple[str, Optional[str]]:
   886→    """Determine provider + model for a call.
   887→
   888→    Priority:
   889→      1. Explicit provider/model args (always win)
   890→      2. Env var overrides (AUXILIARY_{TASK}_PROVIDER, etc.)
   891→      3. Config file (auxiliary.{task}.provider/model or compression.*)
   892→      4. "auto" (full auto-detection chain)
   893→
   894→    Returns (provider, model) where model may be None (use provider default).
   895→    """
   896→    if provider:
   897→        return provider, model
   898→
   899→    if task:
   900→        # Check env var overrides first
   901→        env_provider = _get_auxiliary_provider(task)
   902→        if env_provider != "auto":
   903→            # Check for env var model override too
   904→            env_model = None

536→def resolve_provider_client(
   537→    provider: str,
   538→    model: str = None,
   539→    async_mode: bool = False,
   540→    raw_codex: bool = False,
   541→) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
   542→    """Central router: given a provider name and optional model, return a
   543→    configured client with the correct auth, base URL, and API format.
   544→
   545→    The returned client always exposes ``.chat.completions.create()`` — for
   546→    Codex/Responses API providers, an adapter handles the translation
   547→    transparently.
   548→
   549→    Args:
   550→        provider: Provider identifier.  One of:
   551→            "openrouter", "nous", "openai-codex" (or "codex"),
   552→            "zai", "kimi-coding", "minimax", "minimax-cn",
   553→            "custom" (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY),
   554→            "auto" (full auto-detection chain).
   555→        model: Model slug override.  If None, uses the provider's default
   556→               auxiliary model.
   557→        async_mode: If True, return an async-compatible client.
   558→        raw_codex: If True, return a raw OpenAI client for Codex providers
   559→            instead of wrapping in CodexAuxiliaryClient.  Use this when
   560→            the caller needs direct access to responses.stream() (e.g.,
   561→            the main agent loop).
   562→
   563→    Returns:
   564→        (client, resolved_model) or (None, None) if auth is unavailable.
   565→    """
   566→    # Normalise aliases
   567→    provider = (provider or "auto").strip().lower()
   568→    if provider == "codex":
   569→        provider = "openai-codex"
   570→    if provider == "main":
   571→        provider = "custom"
   572→
   573→    # ── Auto: try all providers in priority order ────────────────────
   574→    if provider == "auto":
   575→        client, resolved = _resolve_auto()
   576→        if client is None:
   577→            return None, None
   578→        final_model = model or resolved
   579→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   580→                else (client, final_model))
   581→
   582→    # ── OpenRouter ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   583→    if provider == "openrouter":
   584→        client, default = _try_openrouter()
   585→        if client is None:
   586→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openrouter requested "
   587→                           "but OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set")
   588→            return None, None
   589→        final_model = model or default
   590→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   591→                else (client, final_model))
   592→
   593→    # ── Nous Portal (OAuth) ──────────────────────────────────────────
   594→    if provider == "nous":
   595→        client, default = _try_nous()
   596→        if client is None:
   597→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: nous requested "
   598→                           "but Nous Portal not configured (run: hermes login)")
   599→            return None, None
   600→        final_model = model or default
   601→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   602→                else (client, final_model))
   603→
   604→    # ── OpenAI Codex (OAuth → Responses API) ─────────────────────────
   605→    if provider == "openai-codex":
   606→        if raw_codex:
   607→            # Return the raw OpenAI client for callers that need direct
   608→            # access to responses.stream() (e.g., the main agent loop).
   609→            [REDACTED]()
   610→            if not codex_token:
   611→                logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openai-codex requested "
   612→                               "but no Codex OAuth token found (run: hermes model)")
   613→                return None, None
   614→            final_model = model or _CODEX_AUX_MODEL
   615→            raw_client = OpenAI(api_key=codex_token, base_url=_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL)
   616→            return (raw_client, final_model)
   617→        # Standard path: wrap in CodexAuxiliaryClient adapter
   618→        client, default = _try_codex()
   619→        if client is None:
   620→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: openai-codex requested "
   621→                           "but no Codex OAuth token found (run: hermes model)")
   622→            return None, None
   623→        final_model = model or default
   624→        return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   625→                else (client, final_model))
   626→
   627→    # ── Custom endpoint (OPENAI_BASE_URL + OPENAI_API_KEY) ───────────
   628→    if provider == "custom":
   629→        # Try custom first, then codex, then API-key providers
   630→        for try_fn in (_try_custom_endpoint, _try_codex,
   631→                       _resolve_api_key_provider):
   632→            client, default = try_fn()
   633→            if client is not None:
   634→                final_model = model or default
   635→                return (_to_async_client(client, final_model) if async_mode
   636→                        else (client, final_model))
   637→        logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: custom/main requested "
   638→                       "but no endpoint credentials found")
   639→        return None, None
   640→
   641→    # ── API-key providers from PROVIDER_REGISTRY ─────────────────────
   642→    try:
   643→        from hermes_cli.auth import PROVIDER_REGISTRY, _resolve_kimi_base_url
   644→    except ImportError:
   645→        logger.debug("hermes_cli.auth not available for provider %s", provider)
   646→        return None, None
   647→
   648→    pconfig = PROVIDER_REGISTRY.get(provider)
   649→    if pconfig is None:
   650→        logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: unknown provider %r", provider)
   651→        return None, None
   652→
   653→    if pconfig.auth_type == "api_key":
   654→        # Find the first configured API key
   655→        api_key = ""
   656→        for env_var in pconfig.api_key_env_vars:
   657→            api_key = os.getenv(env_var, "").strip()
   658→            if api_key:
   659→                break
   660→        if not api_key:
   661→            logger.warning("resolve_provider_client: provider %s has no API "
   662→                           "key configured (tried: %s)",
   663→                           provider, ", ".join(pconfig.api_key_env_vars))
   664→            return None, None
   665→
   666→        # Resolve base URL (env override → provider-specific logic → default)
   667→        base_url_override = os.getenv(pconfig.base_url_env_var, "").strip() if pconfig.base_url_env_var else ""
   668→        if provider == "kimi-coding":
   669→            base_url = _resolve_kimi_base_url(api_key, pconfig.inference_base_url, base_url_override)
   670→        elif base_url_override:
   671→            base_url = base_url_override
   672→        else:
   673→            base_url = pconfig.inference_base_url
   674→
   675→        default_model = _API_KEY_PROVIDER_AUX_MODELS.get(provider, "")
   676→        final_model = model or default_model
   677→
   678→        # Provider-specific headers
   679→        headers = {}
   680→        if "api.kimi.com" in base_url.lower():
   681→            headers["User-Agent"] = "KimiCLI/1.0"
   682→
   683→        client = OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url,
   684→                        **({"default_headers": headers} if headers else {}))
   685→        logger.debug("resolve_provider_client: %s (%s)", provider, final_model)

  2387→    def _run_codex_stream(self, api_kwargs: dict):
  2388→        """Execute one streaming Responses API request and return the final response."""
  2389→        max_stream_retries = 1
  2390→        for attempt in range(max_stream_retries + 1):
  2391→            try:
  2392→                with self.client.responses.stream(**api_kwargs) as stream:
  2393→                    for _ in stream:
  2394→                        pass
  2395→                    return stream.get_final_response()
  2396→            except RuntimeError as exc:
  2397→                err_text = str(exc)
  2398→                missing_completed = "response.completed" in err_text
  2399→                if missing_completed and attempt < max_stream_retries:
  2400→                    logger.debug(
  2401→                        "Responses stream closed before completion (attempt %s/%s); retrying.",
  2402→                        attempt + 1,
  2403→                        max_stream_retries + 1,
  2404→                    )
  2405→                    continue
  2406→                if missing_completed:
  2407→                    logger.debug(
  2408→                        "Responses stream did not emit response.completed; falling back to create(stream=True)."
  2409→                    )
  2410→                    return self._run_codex_create_stream_fallback(api_kwargs)
  2411→                raise
  2412→
  2413→    def _run_codex_create_stream_fallback(self, api_kwargs: dict):
  2414→        """Fallback path for stream completion edge cases on Codex-style Responses backends."""
  2415→        fallback_kwargs = dict(api_kwargs)
  2416→        fallback_kwargs["stream"] = True
  2417→        fallback_kwargs = self._preflight_codex_api_kwargs(fallback_kwargs, allow_stream=True)
  2418→        stream_or_response = self.client.responses.create(**fallback_kwargs)
  2419→
  2420→        # Compatibility shim for mocks or providers that still return a concrete response.
  2421→        if hasattr(stream_or_response, "output"):
  2422→            return stream_or_response
  2423→        if not hasattr(stream_or_response, "__iter__"):
  2424→            return stream_or_response
  2425→
  2426→        terminal_response = None
  2427→        try:
  2428→            for event in stream_or_response:
  2429→                event_type = getattr(event, "type", None)
  2430→                if not event_type and isinstance(event, dict):
  2431→                    event_type = event.get("type")
  2432→                if event_type not in {"response.completed", "response.incomplete", "response.failed"}:
  2433→                    continue
  2434→
  2435→                terminal_response = getattr(event, "response", None)
  2436→                if terminal_response is None and isinstance(event, dict):
  2437→                    terminal_response = event.get("response")
  2438→                if terminal_response is not None:
  2439→                    return terminal_response
  2440→        finally:
  2441→            close_fn = getattr(stream_or_response, "close", None)
  2442→            if callable(close_fn):
  2443→                try:
  2444→                    close_fn()
  2445→                except Exception:
  2446→                    pass
  2447→
  2448→        if terminal_response is not None:
  2449→            return terminal_response
  2450→        raise RuntimeError("Responses create(stream=True) fallback did not emit a terminal response.")

```
# Segfault Root Cause Analysis: openai-codex Provider in ThreadPoolExecutor

## Key Finding
**The segfault is caused by unsynchronized access to a global, thread-unsafe client cache when multiple child agents spawn in ThreadPoolExecutor and attempt to access auxiliary LLM clients concurrently.**

## Critical Issues

### 1. Global Client Cache is Not Thread-Safe
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py`
**Lines:** 863-878

```python
# Client cache: (provider, async_mode) -> (client, default_model)
_client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}

def _get_cached_client(
    provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False,
) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
    """Get or create a cached client for the given provider."""
    cache_key = (provider, async_mode)
    if cache_key in _client_cache:  # ← RACE CONDITION: No lock!
        cached_client, cached_default = _client_cache[cache_key]
        return cached_client, model or cached_default
    client, default_model = resolve_provider_client(provider, model, async_mode)
    if client is not None:
        _client_cache[cache_key] = (client, default_model)  # ← RACE CONDITION
    return client, model or default_model
```

**Problem:**
- Multiple threads can simultaneously check `if cache_key in _client_cache` 
- Both threads see cache miss and call `resolve_provider_client()`
- Both threads then try to store in the dict simultaneously
- For OpenAI clients with httpx transport: concurrent access to the underlying httpx.Client causes corruption

### 2. OpenAI Client Uses Non-Thread-Safe httpx.Client
**Root Cause:**
- OpenAI SDK uses `httpx.Client` internally (not `httpx.AsyncClient`)
- `httpx.Client` is NOT thread-safe - it uses a single TCP connection pool with state
- When multiple threads access the same `httpx.Client`, concurrent socket I/O operations cause memory corruption
- This manifests as `kevent` segfault (macOS event loop kernel call failing)

### 3. Thread Pool Executor Creates Multiple Child Agents with openai-codex Provider
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/delegate_tool.py`
**Lines:** 472-534 (batch delegation with ThreadPoolExecutor)

```python
with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=MAX_CONCURRENT_CHILDREN) as executor:
    futures = {}
    for i, t in enumerate(task_list):
        task_creds = _resolve_task_credentials(t, creds, parent_agent)
        future = executor.submit(
            _run_single_child,  # Each child runs in its own thread
            # ... credentials passed including api_mode="codex_responses"
        )
```

**Problem:**
- Each child agent in a separate thread
- Child agents may call `call_llm()` for auxiliary tasks (context compression, session search, etc.)
- All auxiliary calls go through `_get_cached_client()` → global cache race condition

### 4. Auxiliary Client Caching is Used Throughout Run_agent.py
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py`
**Line:** 3035

```python
from agent.auxiliary_client import call_llm as _call_llm
# ... inside run_conversation or other methods
response = _call_llm(
    task="compression",  # or other tasks
    # ... this calls _get_cached_client() with no synchronization
)
```

Used for:
- Context compression (summary_provider)
- Vision analysis
- Web extraction  
- Session search
- Memory flush

### 5. Specific Issue with Codex Provider + Responses API
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py`
**Line:** 2392

```python
def _run_codex_stream(self, api_kwargs: dict):
    """Execute one streaming Responses API request and return the final response."""
    try:
        with self.client.responses.stream(**api_kwargs) as stream:  # ← httpx transport
            for _ in stream:
                pass
            return stream.get_final_response()
```

**Why Codex makes it worse:**
- Codex uses Responses API streaming endpoint
- Requires multiple HTTP requests per inference
- More file descriptor activity = more kevent calls
- httpx event loop corruption more likely to manifest

This is classic httpx corruption: kevent file descriptor operations fail because the underlying httpx.Client's state is corrupted by concurrent access.

## Timeline of Failure
1. Parent agent starts
2. `delegate_task` called with 3 subagents
3. ThreadPoolExecutor creates threads for each subagent
4. Each child AIAgent initializes (line 219 in delegate_tool.py)
5. Child 1 runs some work, needs context compression
6. Child 1 calls `call_llm(task="compression")`
7. `call_llm` calls `_get_cached_client("auto", ...)`  [line 1022]
8. Cache miss, calls `resolve_provider_client("auto")` [line 875]
9. Thread is pre-empted
10. Child 2 & 3 call `call_llm` simultaneously
11. Race condition: all 3 threads check cache at same time
12. All 3 miss cache, all 3 call `resolve_provider_client` 
13. If codex_responses path is taken:
    - `_try_codex()` creates new OpenAI client [line 457]
    - Client uses new httpx.Client transport
    - BUT if cache hit happens, they share same httpx.Client
14. Concurrent httpx.Client usage → kevent corruption → SIGSEGV

## Affected Code Paths

### Direct Hit:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:863-878` (_client_cache)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:867-878` (_get_cached_client)

### Call Sites (using cached clients in threads):
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:1022` (call_llm)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:1071` (async_call_llm)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py:3035` (call_llm usage)

### Codex-Specific Vulnerability:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:452-458` (_try_codex)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:729-746` (resolve_provider_credentials returns raw Codex client)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py:2392` (_run_codex_stream uses responses.stream())

### Delegation Thread Pool:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/delegate_tool.py:472` (ThreadPoolExecutor)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/delegate_tool.py:219-244` (child AIAgent creation in threads)

## Summary of Causes

1. **Root Cause:** Global `_client_cache` dictionary accessed without locks
2. **Amplifier:** OpenAI SDK uses thread-unsafe httpx.Client
3. **Trigger:** Multiple threads from ThreadPoolExecutor
4. **Manifestation:** kevent corruption → SIGSEGV in select_kqueue_control

The fix requires:
- Thread-safe cache (threading.Lock)
- OR per-thread clients
- OR disable caching in delegate_tool subagents

## Investigation Summary: Hermes Agent Segfault with openai-codex Provider

I've thoroughly investigated the segfault that occurs during subagent delegation with the openai-codex provider. The crash happens when the spinner shows "delegating 3 tasks" and threads in ThreadPoolExecutor attempt concurrent API access.

### Root Cause: Thread-Unsafe Global Client Cache

The **primary root cause** is a race condition in the global client cache in `auxiliary_client.py` combined with the thread-unsafe nature of the OpenAI SDK's httpx transport:

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:863-878`

The `_client_cache` dictionary is accessed without synchronization:
```python
_client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}

def _get_cached_client(provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False):
    cache_key = (provider, async_mode)
    if cache_key in _client_cache:  # RACE CONDITION - no lock
        cached_client, cached_default = _client_cache[cache_key]
        return cached_client, model or cached_default
    client, default_model = resolve_provider_client(provider, model, async_mode)
    if client is not None:
        _client_cache[cache_key] = (client, default_model)  # RACE CONDITION
```

When multiple threads from ThreadPoolExecutor simultaneously check the cache:
1. Thread A checks cache, cache miss
2. Thread B checks cache, cache miss (both threads see the same miss)
3. Both threads call `resolve_provider_client()`
4. Both create new OpenAI clients with httpx.Client transports
5. If a cache hit occurs, they may share the same httpx.Client
6. Concurrent access to httpx.Client → memory corruption → SIGSEGV

### Secondary Issue: Codex Provider Amplifies the Problem

The crash happens specifically with `openai-codex` provider because:

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:452-458`

```python
def _try_codex() -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
    [REDACTED]()
    if not codex_token:
        return None, None
    logger.debug("Auxiliary client: Codex OAuth (%s via Responses API)", _CODEX_AUX_MODEL)
    real_client = OpenAI(api_key=codex_token, base_url=_CODEX_AUX_BASE_URL)
    return CodexAuxiliaryClient(real_client, _CODEX_AUX_MODEL), _CODEX_AUX_MODEL
```

The Codex provider uses the Responses API (`responses.stream()`), which performs multiple concurrent HTTP operations and file descriptor manipulation. This heavily exercises the httpx event loop (kevent on macOS), making the corruption more likely to manifest.

### Affected Code Paths

**Direct race condition point:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:867-878` (`_get_cached_client` function)

**Cache access sites (called from child threads):**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:1022` (in `call_llm`)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:1071` (in `async_call_llm`)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py:3035` (where `call_llm` is invoked)

**Thread pool triggering the issue:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/delegate_tool.py:472-534` (ThreadPoolExecutor batch mode)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/tools/delegate_tool.py:219-244` (child AIAgent initialization in threads)

**Codex-specific vulnerable paths:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/agent/auxiliary_client.py:729-746` (`resolve_provider_credentials` returns raw Codex clients)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/run_agent.py:2392` (`_run_codex_stream` uses `responses.stream()`)

### Crash Log Evidence

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports/python3.11-2026-03-13-203815.ips`:

```
Exception: EXC_BAD_ACCESS (SIGSEGV)
Signal: KERN_INVALID_ADDRESS at 0x6f635f6e656b6f74 (possible pointer authentication failure)
Faulting Thread: 9 (a ThreadPoolExecutor worker)
```

Stack trace shows:
- `kevent` (macOS kernel event loop system call)
- `select_kqueue_control` (socket event handling)
- `_PyEval_EvalFrameDefault` (Python code execution)

This is classic httpx memory corruption: the httpx.Client's internal state (socket pool, file descriptors, event loop) becomes corrupted when accessed concurrently from multiple threads.

### Why This Happens During Delegation

1. Parent agent calls `delegate_task()` with multiple tasks
2. ThreadPoolExecutor spawns 3 worker threads (MAX_CONCURRENT_CHILDREN = 3)
3. Each child AIAgent runs in its own thread
4. Children perform work that triggers auxiliary LLM calls:
   - Context compression (`call_llm(task="compression")`)
   - Session search
   - Memory operations
5. All auxiliary calls go through `_get_cached_client()` with no synchronization
6. Race condition triggers → httpx corruption → SIGSEGV

The specific timing ("delegating 3 tasks" message) matches when all 3 threads are most likely to call `_get_cached_client()` simultaneously.

### Files and Line Numbers Summary

| File | Lines | Issue |
|------|-------|-------|
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 863-878 | Global `_client_cache` without lock (race condition) |
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 867-878 | `_get_cached_client()` not thread-safe |
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 1022 | `call_llm()` calls unsafe cache getter |
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 1071 | `async_call_llm()` calls unsafe cache getter |
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 452-458 | `_try_codex()` creates new clients without sync |
| `agent/auxiliary_client.py` | 729-746 | `resolve_provider_credentials()` returns unguarded clients |
| `run_agent.py` | 3035 | `call_llm()` invoked from threads |
| `run_agent.py` | 2392 | `_run_codex_stream()` uses responses.stream() (high-contention I/O) |
| `tools/delegate_tool.py` | 472-534 | ThreadPoolExecutor with no client cache isolation |
| `tools/delegate_tool.py` | 219-244 | Child AIAgent creation in threads |

The fix requires thread-safe access to the client cache - either via a `threading.Lock`, per-thread clients, or disabling the cache for subagents in delegate_tool.
